CENSUS-BEGIN Sanctions.boxTransfer_existsUnique_invariant_attracting
KIND theorem
BINDER 1 | δ | explicit | false | Real | ℝ
BINDER 2 | h | explicit | false | Real | ℝ
BINDER 3 | hh | explicit | true | LT.lt | 0 < h
BINDER 4 | hδ0 | explicit | true | LT.lt | 0 < δ
BINDER 5 | hδ1 | explicit | true | LT.lt | δ < 1
CONCL ∃ lam, (MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure lam ∧ Sanctions.boxTransfer δ h lam = lam) ∧ (∀ (ν : MeasureTheory.Measure ↑Sanctions.S), MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure ν → Sanctions.boxTransfer δ h ν = ν → ν = lam) ∧ ∀ (μ0 : MeasureTheory.Measure ↑Sanctions.S), MeasureTheory.IsProbabilityMeasure μ0 → ∀ (n : ℕ), Sanctions.dualKR ((Sanctions.boxTransfer δ h)^[n] μ0) lam ≤ (1 - δ) ^ n * Sanctions.dualKR μ0 lam
CENSUS-END
